text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = ' '.join(text.split())\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_reason(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw reason text into meaningful spans\n        Split by delimiters: , ; & / + 、\n        Also handle patterns like \"...已处理\", \"...有问题\", \"...重测过\", \"...OK了\"\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text:\n            return []\n\n        # Replace various delimiters with a common separator\n        text = raw_text\n        # Handle Chinese punctuation and common separators\n        separators = [' & ', ' ; ', ' / ', ' + ', '、', '，', '；']\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the common separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # Further clean segments - remove trailing status words\n        cleaned_segments = []\n        status_suffixes = ['已处理', '有问题', '重测过', 'OK了', '不稳定', '返修', '疑似', '接触不好', 'NG']\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Remove status suffixes\n            cleaned = seg\n            for suffix in status_suffixes:\n                if cleaned.endswith(suffix):\n                    cleaned = cleaned[:-len(suffix)].strip()\n\n            if cleaned and len(cleaned) > 2:  # Avoid very short meaningless segments\n                cleaned_segments.append(cleaned)\n\n        # If no segments after cleaning, return original split\n        if not cleaned_segments:\n            cleaned_segments = [s for s in segments if len(s) > 2]\n\n        return cleaned_segments if cleaned_segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract important keywords from text\"\"\"\n        keywords = []\n\n        # Common test-related keywords (English and Chinese)\n        patterns = {\n            'open': r'\\b(open|开路|OS)\\b',\n            'short': r'\\b(short|短路|bridge|桥连|连锡)\\b',\n            'cold_solder': r'\\b(cold\\s*solder|虚焊|浮高|少锡|missing\\s*solder)\\b',\n            'fixture': r'\\b(fixture|治具|探针|pogo|pin|reseat|接触不良|接触差|接觸不良)\\b',\n            'flash': r'\\b(flash|program|烧录|写入|write|reflash)\\b',\n            'current': r'\\b(current|电流|ICC|功耗)\\b',\n            'checksum': r'\\b(checksum|CRC|校验|verify)\\b',\n            'boot': r'\\b(boot|OS\\s*fail|启动)\\b',\n            'probe': r'\\b(probe\\s*worn|探针磨损|换针)\\b',\n            'intermittent': r'\\b(intermittent|不稳|unstable)\\b',\n            'component': r'\\b(component|器件|异常)\\b',\n            'version': r'\\b(version|版本不符|wrong\\s*FW)\\b',\n        }\n\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        for key, pattern in patterns.items():\n            if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):\n                keywords.append(key)\n\n        # Extract component/net names (e.g., R152, U12_VDD, SPI_MISO)\n        comp_pattern = r'\\b([RUCLDTJQY]\\d+[A-Z_]*|[A-Z]+_[A-Z_0-9]+)\\b'\n        components = re.findall(comp_pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        return SequenceMatcher(None,\n                             self.normalize_text(text1),\n                             self.normalize_text(text2)).ratio()\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate match score based on keyword matching\n        \"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(segment))\n\n        # Get keywords from codebook\n        cb_keywords_str = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n        cb_label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords and label for matching\n        cb_text = f\"{cb_keywords_str} {cb_label}\".lower()\n        cb_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(cb_text))\n\n        if not segment_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & cb_keywords\n        union = segment_keywords | cb_keywords\n\n        if not union:\n            return 0.0\n\n        jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union)\n\n        # Bonus for exact component/net name matches\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label_upper = cb_label.upper()\n\n        comp_bonus = 0.0\n        comp_pattern = r'\\b([RUCLDTJQY]\\d+[A-Z_]*|[A-Z]+_[A-Z_0-9]+)\\b'\n segment_comps & label_comps:\n                comp_bonus = 0.3\n\n        return min(1.0, jaccard + comp_bonus)\n\n    def match_segment_to_codebook(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Match a segment to the best codebook entry\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.1, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Check station compatibility\n            station_scope = entry.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station and station not in station_scope:\n                continue  # Skip if station doesn't match\n\n            # Calculate multiple similarity scores\n            keyword_score = self.keyword_match_score(segment, entry)\n            label_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['standard_label'])\n            keywords_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['keywords_examples'])\n\n            # Weighted combination\n            combined_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.5 +\n                label_similarity * 0.3 +\n                keywords_similarity * 0.2\n            )\n\n            if combined_score > best_score:\n                best_score = combined_score\n CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD:\n            # Station validation bonus\n            station_match = station in best_match.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station_match:\n                confidence = min(0.95, best_score + 0.1)\n            else:\n                confidence = best_score * 0.8  # Penalize if station doesn't match\n\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{best_match['standard_label']}' with score {best_score:.3f}\"\n            if station_match:\n                rationale += f\"; station '{station}' validated\"\n\n            return (\n                best_match['code'],\n                best_match['standard_label'],\n                round(confidence, 3),\n                rationale\n            )\n        else:\n            # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            confidence = round(best_score * 0.5, 3) if best_score > 0 else 0.05\n            rationale = f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n            if best_match:\n                rationale += f\"; closest: '{best_match['standard_label']}'\"\n\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n print(f\"  Progress: {i}/{len(records)}\")\n\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(record)\n            results.append(normalized_record)\n\n        return {\"records\": results}\n\n Common test-related keywords (English and Chinese)\n        patterns = {\n            'open': r'(open|开路|OS|断路)',\n            'short': r'(short|短路|bridge|桥连|连锡|短露)',\n            'cold_solder': r'(cold\\s*solder|虚焊|浮高|少锡|missing\\s*solder|接触|contact)',\n            'fixture': r'(fixture|治具|探针|pogo|pin|reseat|接触不良|接触差|接觸不良|unstable|重插)',\n            'flash': r'(flash|program|烧录|写入|write|reflash|programming)',\n            'current': r'(current|电流|ICC|功耗|idle.*current|静态电流|consumption)',\n            'checksum': r'(checksum|CRC|校验|verify|校驗)',\n            'boot': r'(boot|OS\\s*fail|启动|stuck)',\n            'probe': r'(probe\\s*worn|探针磨损|换针|worn|磨损)',\n            'intermittent': r'(intermittent|不稳|unstable|功能不稳)',\n            'component': r'(component|器件|异常|issue|damage)',\n            'version': r'(version|版本不符|wrong\\s*FW|mismatch|版本)',\n            'leakage': r'(leakage|漏电|hipot|绝缘)',\n            'missing': r'(missing|少件|缺件)',\n            'reversed': r'(reversed|polarity|反向|极性)',\n            'pressure': r'(pressure|压伤|pad\\s*damage)',\n            'esd': r'(ESD|静电)',\n            'contamination': r'(contamination|residue|助焊剂|清洗)',\n            'connector': r'(connector|插拔|wear)',\n            'cal_data': r'(cal\\s*data|calibration|校准|RF\\s*cal)',\n        }\n\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        for key, pattern in patterns.items():\n            if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):\n                keywords.append(key)\n\n        # List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw reason text into meaningful spans\n        Split by delimiters: , ; & / + 、\n        Also handle patterns like \"...已处理\", \"...有问题\", \"...重测过\", \"...OK了\"\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text:\n            return []\n\n        # Replace various delimiters with a common separator\n        text = raw_text\n        # Handle Chinese punctuation and common separators\n        separators = [' & ', ' ; ', ' / ', ' + ', '、', '，', '；']\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the common separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # Further clean segments - remove trailing status words\n        cleaned_segments = []\n        status_suffixes = ['已处理', '有问题', '重测过', 'OK了', '不稳定', '返修', '疑似', '接触不好', 'NG']\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Remove status suffixes\n            cleaned = seg\n            for suffix in status_suffixes:\n                if cleaned.endswith(suffix):\n                    cleaned = cleaned[:-len(suffix)].strip()\n\n            if cleaned and len(cleaned) > 2:  # Avoid very short meaningless segments\n                cleaned_segments.append(cleaned)\n\n        # If no segments after cleaning, return original split\n        if not cleaned_segments:\n            cleaned_segments         'flash': r'\\b(flash|program|烧录|写入|write|reflash)\\b',\n            'current': r'\\b(current|电流|ICC|功耗)\\b',\n            'checksum': r'\\b(checksum|CRC|校验|verify)\\b',\n            'boot':                             self.normalize_text(text1),\n                             self.normalize_text(text2)).ratio()\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate match score based on keyword matching\n        \"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(segment))\n\n        # Get keywords from codebook\n        cb_keywords_str = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n        cb_label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords and label for # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & cb_keywords\n        union = segment_keywords | cb_keywords\n\n        if not union:\n            return 0.0\n\n \"\"\"\n        Match a segment to the best codebook entry\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n     rationale = f\"Matched to '{best_match['standard_label']}' with score {best_score:.3f}\"\n            if station_match:\n                rationale += f\"; station '{station}' validated\"\n\n            return (\n                best_match['code'],\n                best_match['standard_label'],\n                round(confidence, 3),\n                rationale\n            )\n        else:\n            # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            confidence = round(best_score * 0.5, 3) if best_score > 0 else 0.05\n            rationale = f\"No confident match found (best score: \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        products = ['P1_POWER', 'P2_CTRL', 'P3_RF']\n        for product in products:\n            filepath = f'/app/data/codebook_{product}.csv'\n self.codebooks[product].append(row)\n\n    def normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for better matching\"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = ' '.join(text.split())\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_reason(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw reason text into meaningful spans\n        Split by delimiters: , ; & / + 、\n        Also handle patterns like \"...已处理\", \"...有问题\", \"...重测过\", \"...OK了\"\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text:\n            return []\n\n        # Replace various delimiters with a common separator\n        text = raw_text\n        # Handle Chinese punctuation and common separators\n        separators = [' & ', ' ; ', ' / ', ' + ', '、', '，', '；']\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the common separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # Further clean segments - remove trailing status words\n        cleaned_segments = []\n        status_suffixes = ['已处理', '有问题', '重测过', 'OK了', '不稳定', '返修', '疑似', '接触不好', 'NG']\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Remove status suffixes\n            cleaned = seg\n            for suffix in status_suffixes:\n                if cleaned.endswith(suffix):\n                    cleaned = cleaned[:-len(suffix)].strip()\n\n            if cleaned and len(cleaned) > 2:  # Avoid very short meaningless segments\n                cleaned_segments.append(cleaned)\n\n        # If no segments after cleaning, return original split\n        if not cleaned_segments:\n            cleaned_segments = [s for s in segments if len(s) > 2]\n\n        return cleaned_segments if cleaned_segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract important         'cal_data': r'(cal\\s*data|calibration|校准|RF\\s*cal)',\n        }\n\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        for key, pattern in patterns.items():\n            if re.search(pattern, Normalize component names: R152_NET -> R152\n        normalized_comps = []\n        for comp in components:\n            comp_upper = comp.upper()\n            # Extract base component (e.g., R152 float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        return SequenceMatcher(None,\n                             self.normalize_text(text1),\n \"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(segment))\n\n        # Get keywords from codebook\n        cb_keywords_str = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n        cb_label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords and label for matching\n        cb_text = f\"{cb_keywords_str} {cb_label}\".lower()\n        cb_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(cb_text))\n\n        if not segment_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & cb_keywords\n scores\n            keyword_score = self.keyword_match_score(segment, entry)\n            label_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['standard_label'])\n            keywords_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['keywords_examples'])\n\n            # Weighted combination\n            combined_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.5 +\n                label_similarity * 0.3 +\n                keywords_similarity * 0.2\n            )\n\n            if combined_score > best_score:\n                best_score = combined_score\n CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD:\n            # Station validation bonus\n            station_match = station in best_match.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station_match:\n                confidence = min(0.95, best_score + 0.1)\n            else:\n                confidence = best_score * 0.8  # Penalize if station doesn't match\n\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{best_match['standard_label']}' with score {best_score:.3f}\"\n            if station_match:\n                rationale += f\"; station '{station}' validated\"\n\n            return (\n                best_match['code'],\n                best_match['standard_label'],\n                round(confidence, 3),\n                rationale\n            )\n        else:\n            # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            confidence = round(best_score * 0.5, 3) if best_score > 0 else 0.05\n            rationale = f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n            if best_match:\n                rationale += f\"; closest: '{best_match['standard_label']}'\"\n\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record:   \"\"\"Normalize text for better matching\"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = ' '.join(text.split())\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_reason(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw reason text into meaningful spans\n        Split by delimiters: , ; & / + 、\n        Also handle patterns like \"...已处理\", \"...有问题\", \"...重测过\", \"...OK了\"\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text:\n            return []\n\n        # Replace various delimiters with a common separator\n        text = raw_text\n        # Handle Chinese punctuation and common separators\n        separators = [' & ', ' ; ', ' / ', ' + ', '、', '，', '；']\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the common separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # Further clean segments - remove trailing status words\n        cleaned_segments = []\n        status_suffixes = ['已处理', '有问题', '重测过', 'OK了', '不稳定', '返修', '疑似', '接触不好', 'NG']\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Remove status suffixes\n            cleaned = seg\n            for suffix in status_suffixes:\n                if cleaned.endswith(suffix):\n                    cleaned = cleaned[:-len(suffix)].strip()\n\n            if cleaned and len(cleaned) > 2:  # Avoid very short meaningless segments\n                cleaned_segments.append(cleaned)\n\n        # If no segments after cleaning, return original split\n        if not cleaned_segments:\n            cleaned_segments = [s for s in segments if len(s) > 2]\n\n        return cleaned_segments if cleaned_segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract important keywords from text\"\"\"\n        keywords = []\n\n        # Common test-related keywords (English and Chinese)\n        patterns = {\n            'open': r'(open|开路|OS|断路)',\n            'short': r'(short|短路|bridge|桥连|连锡|短露)',\n            'cold_solder': 'boot': r'(boot|OS\\s*fail|启动|stuck)',\n            'probe': r'(probe\\s*worn|探针磨损|换针|worn|磨损)',\n            'intermittent': r'(intermittent|不稳|unstable|功能不稳)',\n            'component': names (e.g., R152, U12_VDD, SPI_MISO)\n        # Also handle variations like R152_NET should match R152\n        comp_pattern = -> R152\n        normalized_comps = []\n        for comp in components:\n            comp_upper = comp.upper()\n            # Extract base component (e.g., R152 from R152_NET)\n            base = normalized_comps.append(comp_upper)\n\n        keywords.extend(normalized_comps[:5])  # Limit to top 5\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def calculate_similarity(self, text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        return SequenceMatcher(None,\n                             self.normalize_text(text1),\n                             self.normalize_text(text2)).ratio()\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate match score based on keyword matching\n        \"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(segment))\n\n        # Get keywords from codebook\n        cb_keywords_str = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n        cb_label comp_penalty = 0.0\n        if segment_comps and label_comps and not (segment_comps & label_comps):\n            comp_penalty = 0.2\n\n        return max(0.0, min(1.0, weighted_score + comp_bonus - comp_penalty))\n\n    def match_segment_to_codebook(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.1, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Check station compatibility\n            station_scope = entry.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station and station not in station_scope:\n                continue  # Skip if station doesn't match\n\n            # Calculate multiple similarity scores\n            keyword_score = self.keyword_match_score(segment, entry)\n            label_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['standard_label'])\n            keywords_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['keywords_examples'])\n\n            # Weighted combination\n            combined_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.5 +\n                label_similarity * 0.3 +\n                keywords_similarity * 0.2\n            )\n\n            if combined_score > best_score:\n                best_score = combined_score\n                best_match = entry\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.35  # Threshold for accepting a match\n\n        if best_match and best_score >= CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD:\n            # Station validation bonus\n            station_match = station in best_match.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station_match:\n                confidence = min(0.95, best_score + 0.1)\n            else:\n                confidence = best_score * 0.8  # Penalize if station doesn't match\n\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{best_match['standard_label']}' with score {best_score:.3f}\"\n            if station_match:\n                rationale += f\"; station '{station}' validated\"\n\n load_codebooks(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        products = ['P1_POWER', 'P2_CTRL', 'P3_RF']\n        for product in products:\n            filepath = f'/app/data/codebook_{product}.csv'\n            self.codebooks[product] = []\n            with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    self.codebooks[product].append(row)\n\n    def normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for better matching\"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = ' '.join(text.split())\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_reason(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw reason text into meaningful spans\n        Split by delimiters: , ; & / + 、\n        Also handle patterns like \"...已处理\", \"...有问题\", \"...重测过\", \"...OK了\"\n        \"\"\"\n        if not raw_text:\n            return []\n\n        # Replace various delimiters with a common separator\n        text = raw_text\n        # Handle Chinese punctuation and common separators\n        separators = [' & ', ' ; ', ' / ', ' + ', '、', '，', '；']\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the common separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # Further clean segments - remove trailing status words\n        cleaned_segments = []\n        status_suffixes = ['已处理', '有问题', '重测过', 'OK了', '不稳定', '返修', '疑似', '接触不好', 'NG']\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Remove status suffixes\n            cleaned = seg\n            for suffix in status_suffixes:\n                if cleaned.endswith(suffix):\n                    cleaned = cleaned[:-len(suffix)].strip()\n\n            if cleaned and len(cleaned) > 2:  # Avoid very short meaningless segments\n                cleaned_segments.append(cleaned)\n\n        # If no segments after cleaning, return original split\n        if not cleaned_segments:\n            cleaned_segments = [s for s in segments if len(s) > 2]\n\n        return cleaned_segments if cleaned_segments else [raw_text]\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract important keywords from text\"\"\"\n        keywords = []\n\n        # Common test-related keywords (English and Chinese)\n        patterns = {\n            'open': r'(open|开路|OS|断路)',\n            'short': r'(short|短路|bridge|桥连|连锡|短露)',\n            'cold_solder': r'(cold\\s*solder|虚焊|浮高|少锡|missing\\s*solder|接触|contact)',\n            'fixture': r'(current|电流|ICC|功耗|idle.*current|静态电流|consumption)',\n            'checksum': r'(checksum|CRC|校验|verify|校驗)',\n            'boot': r'(boot|OS\\s*fail|启动|stuck)',\n            'probe': r'(version|版本不符|wrong\\s*FW|mismatch|版本)',\n            'leakage': r'(leakage|漏电|hipot|绝缘)',\n            'missing': r'(missing|少件|缺件)',\n            'reversed': r'(reversed|polarity|反向|极性)',\n            'pressure': r'(pressure|压伤|pad\\s*damage)',\n            'esd': r'(ESD|静电)',\n            'contamination': r'(contamination|residue|助焊剂|清洗)',\n            'connector': r'(connector|插拔|wear)',\n            'cal_data': r'(cal\\s*data|calibration|校准|RF\\s*cal)',\n        }\n\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        for key, pattern in patterns.items():\n            if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):\n                keywords.append(key)\n\n        # Extract component/net names (e.g., R152, U12_VDD, SPI_MISO)\n        # Also handle variations like R152_NET should match R152\n        comp_pattern = r'\\b([RUCLDTJQYSHANTFBPWX]\\d+[A-Z_]*|[A-Z]+_[A-Z_0-9]+|TP\\d+|ANT\\d+|SH\\d+)\\b'\n        components = re.findall(comp_pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Normalize component names: R152_NET -> R152\n        normalized_comps = []\n        for comp in components:\n            comp_upper = comp.upper()\n            # Extract base component (e.g., R152 from R152_NET)\n            base = re.match(r'^([RUCLDTJQYSHANTFBPWX]\\d+)', comp_upper)\n            if base:\n                normalized_comps.append(base.group(1))\n            else:\n float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate similarity between two texts\"\"\"\n        return SequenceMatcher(None,\n                             self.normalize_text(text1),\n                             self.normalize_text(text2)).ratio()\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate match score based on keyword matching\n        \"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(segment))\n\n        # Get keywords from codebook\n        cb_keywords_str = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n        cb_label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        cb_category = codebook_entry.get('category_lv2', '')\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords and label for matching\n        cb_text = f\"{cb_keywords_str} {cb_label} {cb_category}\".lower()\n        cb_keywords = set(self.extract_keywords(cb_text))\n\n        if not segment_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & cb_keywords\n        union = segment_keywords | cb_keywords\n\n        if not union:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Weighted Jaccard - give more weight to intersection\n        jaccard = len(intersection) / len(union)\n        weighted_score = min(1.0, jaccard * 1.5)  # Boost matches\n\n        # Bonus for exact component/net name matches\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label_upper = cb_label.upper()\n\n        comp_bonus = 0.0\n        comp_pattern = r'([RUCLDTJQYSHANTFBPWX]\\d+)'\n        segment_comps = label_comps:\n                comp_bonus = 0.35  # Strong bonus for component match\n\n        # Penalty for component mismatch (segment has component but label has different one)\n        comp_penalty = 0.0\n        if segment_comps and label_comps and not (segment_comps & label_comps):\n            comp_penalty = 0.2\n\n        return max(0.0, min(1.0, weighted_score + comp_bonus -   Match a segment to the best codebook entry\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.1, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Check station compatibility\n            station_scope = entry.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station and station not in station_scope:\n                continue  # Skip if station doesn't match\n\n            # Calculate multiple similarity scores\n            keyword_score = self.keyword_match_score(segment, entry)\n            label_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['standard_label'])\n            keywords_similarity = self.calculate_similarity(segment, entry['keywords_examples'])\n\n            # Weighted combination\n            combined_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.5 +\n                label_similarity * 0.3 +\n                keywords_similarity * 0.2\n            )\n\n            if combined_score > best_score:\n                best_score = combined_score\n                best_match = entry\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.28  # Threshold for accepting a match (lowered)\n\n        if best_match and best_score >= CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD:\n            # Station validation bonus\n            station_match = station in best_match.get('station_scope', '')\n            if station_match:\n                confidence = min(0.95, best_score + 0.15)\n            else:\n                confidence = best_score * 0.85  # Slight penalize if station doesn't match\n\n            # Cap confidence based on score tiers\n            if best_score < 0.35:\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.65)  # Low-medium confidence\n            elif best_score < 0.50:\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.75)  # Medium confidence\n            else:\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.90)  # High confidence\n\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{best_match['standard_label']}' with score {best_score:.3f}\"\n            if station_match:\n                rationale += f\"; station '{station}' validated\"\n\n            return (\n                best_match['code'],\n                best_match['standard_label'],\n                round(confidence, 3),\n                rationale\n            )\n        else:\n            # Low confidence - mark as UNKNOWN\n            confidence = round(best_score * 0.4, 3) if best_score > 0 else 0.05\n            rationale = f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n            if best_match:\n                rationale += f\"; closest: '{best_match['standard_label']}'\"\n\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        raw_reason = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Segment the raw reason text\n        segments = self.segment_raw_reason(raw_reason)\n\n        # If segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": f\"{record_id}-S{segment_id_counter}\",\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": confidence,\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n            segment_id_counter += 1\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": record['engineer_id'],\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def process_all_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Process all records from test_center_logs.csv\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                records.append(row)\n\n        print(f\"Processing {len(records)} records...\")\n\n        results = []\n        for i, record in enumerate(records):\n            if i % 1000 == 0:\n                print(f\"  Progress: {i}/{len(records)}\")\n\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(record)\n            results.append(normalized_record)\n\n        return {\"records\": results}\n\n    def save_results(self, results: Dict, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Save results to JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(results, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n        print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r    confidences = [\n            seg['confidence']\n            for r in results['records']\n            for seg in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        avg_confidence = sum(confidences) /
